## Client Classes

Client classes are the main entry point to using a package. They contain several variations of Java methods for each of the API's methods. 

Client |  Description  
---|---

The resources currently supported in Business Glossary are:

  1. Glossary 2. GlossaryCategory 3. GlossaryTerm 

  
[com.google.cloud.dataplex.v1.CatalogServiceClient](https://cloud.google.com/java/docs/reference/google-cloud-dataplex/latest/com.google.cloud.dataplex.v1.CatalogServiceClient) |  Service Description: The primary resources offered by this service are EntryGroups, EntryTypes, AspectTypes, Entries and EntryLinks. They collectively let data administrators organize, manage, secure, and catalog data located across cloud projects in their organization in a variety of storage systems, including Cloud Storage and BigQuery.
